cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.10)
project(floydwarshall_benchmark)

set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)
set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)

add_executable(floydwarshall floydwarshall.cpp)

# Enable optimizations
target_compile_options(floydwarshall PRIVATE -O3 -march=native -Wall -Wextra)

# Link math library
target_link_libraries(floydwarshall m)
#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;

// Index calculation for flattened 2D array
inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
    return j * n + i;
}

void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);
    }
    
    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;
    }
}

void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            path[idx2(i, j, numNodes)] = j;
            path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = i;
        }
        path[idx2(j, j, numNodes)] = j;
    }
}

void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                   const size_t numNodes) {
    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
    // For each intermediate node k
    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
        // For each source node i
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            // For each destination node j
            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
                
                if (newDist < distIJ) {
                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
    // Basic sanity checks
    
    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {
            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\n", i, i);
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]
    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time
    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {
            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                // Check for overflow before addition
                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {
                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {
                        printf("Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\n", 
                               i, j, k);
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t numNodes = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of nodes: %zu\n", numNodes);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(numNodes * numNodes);
    std::vector<unsigned int> path(numNodes * numNodes);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing graph...\n");
    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);
    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes);
    
    // Run Floyd-Warshall
    printf("Computing shortest paths...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate operations per second
    // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity
    double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation (integer hash-based)
    if (printResults) {
        print_results_int(dist, "DistanceMatrix");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(dist, numNodes);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;

// Index calculation for flattened 2D array
inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
    return j * n + i;
}

void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);
    }
    
    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;
    }
}

void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            path[idx2(i, j, numNodes)] = j;
            path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = i;
        }
        path[idx2(j, j, numNodes)] = j;
    }
}

void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                   const size_t numNodes) {
    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
    // For each intermediate node k
    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
        // For each source node i
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            // For each destination node j
            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
                
                if (newDist < distIJ) {
                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
    // Basic sanity checks
    
    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {
            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\n", i, i);
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]
    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time
    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {
            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                // Check for overflow before addition
                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {
                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {
                        printf("Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\n", 
                               i, j, k);
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t numNodes = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of nodes: %zu\n", numNodes);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(numNodes * numNodes);
    std::vector<unsigned int> path(numNodes * numNodes);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing graph...\n");
    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);
    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes);
    
    // Run Floyd-Warshall
    printf("Computing shortest paths...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate operations per second
    // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity
    double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation (integer hash-based)
    if (printResults) {
        print_results_int(dist, "DistanceMatrix");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(dist, numNodes);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

codex
The intermediate-node loop must stay ordered to preserve the distance and path matrices. I’ll use a persistent OpenMP team to update separate rows, with SIMD for the inner loop and a barrier between intermediate nodes. The pivot row will remain read-only to avoid races.

 void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                    std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                    const size_t numNodes) {
-    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
-    // For each intermediate node k
-    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
-        // For each source node i
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
-            // For each destination node j
-            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
-                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
-                
-                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
-                
-                if (newDist < distIJ) {
-                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
-                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
+    unsigned int* const distances = dist.data();
+    unsigned int* const paths = path.data();
+
+    // Keep the team alive across all pivots. Each thread owns complete rows;
+    // the worksharing barrier finishes pivot k before any thread starts k + 1.
+    #pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
+            const unsigned int* const pivot = distances + k * numNodes;
+            const unsigned int intermediate = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);
+
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
+                // With nonnegative weights and a zero diagonal, the pivot row
+                // cannot improve. Leaving it untouched makes shared reads safe.
+                if (i == k) {
+                    continue;
+                }
+                unsigned int* const row = distances + i * numNodes;
+                unsigned int* const pathRow = paths + i * numNodes;
+                const unsigned int distIK = row[k];
+
+                #pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
+                    const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivot[j];
+                    if (newDist < row[j]) {
+                        row[j] = newDist;
+                        pathRow[j] = intermediate;
+                    }
                 }
             }
         }

void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        std::fill_n(path.data() + i * numNodes, numNodes,
                    static_cast<unsigned int>(i));
    }
}

void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                   const size_t numNodes) {
    unsigned int* const distances = dist.data();
    unsigned int* const paths = path.data();

    // Keep the team alive across all pivots. Each thread owns complete rows;
    // the worksharing barrier finishes pivot k before any thread starts k + 1.
    #pragma omp parallel
    {
        for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
            const unsigned int* const pivot = distances + k * numNodes;
            const unsigned int intermediate = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);

            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
            for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
                // With nonnegative weights and a zero diagonal, the pivot row
                // cannot improve. Leaving it untouched makes shared reads safe.
                if (i == k) {
                    continue;
                }
                unsigned int* const row = distances + i * numNodes;
                unsigned int* const pathRow = paths + i * numNodes;
                const unsigned int distIK = row[k];

                #pragma omp simd
                for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
                    const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivot[j];
                    if (newDist < row[j]) {
                        row[j] = newDist;
                        pathRow[j] = intermediate;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
    // Basic sanity checks
    
    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {
            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\n", i, i);
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]
    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time
    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {

-        }

+    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
+        std::fill_n(path.data() + i * numNodes, numNodes,
+                    static_cast<unsigned int>(i));

 void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                    std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                    const size_t numNodes) {
-    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
-    // For each intermediate node k
-    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
-        // For each source node i
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
-            // For each destination node j
-            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
-                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
-                
-                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
-                
-                if (newDist < distIJ) {
-                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
-                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
+    unsigned int* const distances = dist.data();
+    unsigned int* const paths = path.data();
+
+    // Keep the team alive across all pivots. Each thread owns complete rows;
+    // the worksharing barrier finishes pivot k before any thread starts k + 1.
+    #pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
+            const unsigned int* const pivot = distances + k * numNodes;
+            const unsigned int intermediate = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);
+
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
+                // With nonnegative weights and a zero diagonal, the pivot row
+                // cannot improve. Leaving it untouched makes shared reads safe.
+                if (i == k) {
+                    continue;
+                }
+                unsigned int* const row = distances + i * numNodes;
+                unsigned int* const pathRow = paths + i * numNodes;
+                const unsigned int distIK = row[k];
+
+                #pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
+                    const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivot[j];
+                    if (newDist < row[j]) {
+                        row[j] = newDist;
+                        pathRow[j] = intermediate;
+                    }
                 }
             }
         }

/bin/bash -lc "python3 - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
source = Path('floydwarshall/floydwarshall.cpp').read_text()
old = '''                    const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivot[j];
                    if (newDist < row[j]) {
                        row[j] = newDist;
                        pathRow[j] = intermediate;
                    }'''
variants = {
    'branchless': '''                    const unsigned int oldDist = row[j];
                    const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivot[j];
                    const unsigned int best = std::min(oldDist, newDist);
                    row[j] = best;
                    pathRow[j] = oldDist "'!= best ? intermediate : pathRow[j];'"''',
    'minmask': '''                    const unsigned int oldDist = row[j];
                    const unsigned int best = std::min(oldDist, distIK + pivot[j]);
                    if (oldDist "'!= best) {
                        row[j] = best;
                        pathRow[j] = intermediate;
                    }'"''',
}
for name, replacement in variants.items():
    subprocess.run(['g++', '-O3', '-march=native', '-std=c++20', '-fopenmp', '-I', 'floydwarshall', '-x', 'c++', '-', '-o', '/tmp/floydwarshall-' + name], input=source.replace(old, replacement), text=True, check=True)

codex
The OpenMP version matches every distance and path entry across small and uneven sizes, dense graphs, zero-weight edges, unreachable edges, and 1–16 threads. It also scales well through 64 threads in the first benchmarks. I’m now comparing SIMD update strategies and checking higher thread counts.

/bin/bash -lc "python3 -u - <<'PY'
import os, re, subprocess
for n in [512, 1024, 2048]:
    for variant in ['openmp', 'branchless', 'minmask']:
        for threads in [1, 16, 64, 128]:
            env = dict(os.environ, OMP_NUM_THREADS=str(threads), OMP_PROC_BIND='close', OMP_PLACES='cores')
            result = subprocess.check_output(['/tmp/floydwarshall-' + variant, '-n', str(n), '-v', '-r'], env=env, text=True, timeout=30)
            timing = re.search(r'Computation time: (\\d+)', result).group(1)
            checksum = re.search(r'Hash: (\\w+)', result).group(1)
            print(f'n={n} {variant} threads={threads}: {timing} ms hash={checksum}', flush=True)

 succeeded in 0ms:
#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include <omp.h>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;

// Index calculation for flattened 2D array
inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
    return j * n + i;
}

void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);
    }
    
    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;
    }
}

void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        std::fill_n(path.data() + i * numNodes, numNodes,
                    static_cast<unsigned int>(i));
    }
}

void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                   const size_t numNodes) {
    unsigned int* const distances = dist.data();
    unsigned int* const paths = path.data();

    // Keep the team alive across all pivots. Each thread owns complete rows;
    // the worksharing barrier finishes pivot k before any thread starts k + 1.
    #pragma omp parallel
    {
        for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
            const unsigned int* const pivot = distances + k * numNodes;
            const unsigned int intermediate = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);

            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
            for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
                // With nonnegative weights and a zero diagonal, the pivot row
                // cannot improve. Leaving it untouched makes shared reads safe.
                if (i == k) {
                    continue;
                }
                unsigned int* const row = distances + i * numNodes;
                unsigned int* const pathRow = paths + i * numNodes;
                const unsigned int distIK = row[k];

                #pragma omp simd
                for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
                    const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivot[j];
                    if (newDist < row[j]) {
                        row[j] = newDist;
                        pathRow[j] = intermediate;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
    // Basic sanity checks
    
    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {
            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\n", i, i);
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]
    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time
    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {
            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                // Check for overflow before addition
                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {
                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {
                        printf("Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\n", 
                               i, j, k);
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t numNodes = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of nodes: %zu\n", numNodes);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(numNodes * numNodes);
    std::vector<unsigned int> path(numNodes * numNodes);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing graph...\n");
    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);
    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes);
    
    // Run Floyd-Warshall
    printf("Computing shortest paths...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate operations per second
    // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity
    double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation (integer hash-based)
    if (printResults) {
        print_results_int(dist, "DistanceMatrix");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(dist, numNodes);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

import os, re, subprocess
for n in [512, 1024, 2048, 4096]:
    for threads in [1, 16, 64, 128, 256]:
        env = dict(os.environ, OMP_NUM_THREADS=str(threads), OMP_PROC_BIND='close', OMP_PLACES='cores')
        result = subprocess.check_output(['/tmp/floydwarshall-pipeline', '-n', str(n), '-v', '-r'], env=env, text=True, timeout=30)

    print(f'n={n} pipeline default: ' + re.search(r'Computation time:.*', result).group(0), flush=True)

 void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                    std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                    const size_t numNodes) {
-    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
-    // For each intermediate node k
-    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
-        // For each source node i
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
-            // For each destination node j
-            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
-                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
-                
-                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
-                
-                if (newDist < distIJ) {
-                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
-                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
+    unsigned int* const distances = dist.data();
+    unsigned int* const paths = path.data();
+
+    // Keep the team alive across all pivots. Each thread owns complete rows;
+    // the worksharing barrier finishes pivot k before any thread starts k + 1.
+    #pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
+            const unsigned int* const pivot = distances + k * numNodes;
+            const unsigned int intermediate = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);
+
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
+                // With nonnegative weights and a zero diagonal, the pivot row
+                // cannot improve. Leaving it untouched makes shared reads safe.
+                if (i == k) {
+                    continue;
+                }
+                unsigned int* const row = distances + i * numNodes;
+                unsigned int* const pathRow = paths + i * numNodes;
+                const unsigned int distIK = row[k];
+
+                #pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
+                    const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivot[j];
+                    if (newDist < row[j]) {
+                        row[j] = newDist;
+                        pathRow[j] = intermediate;
+                    }
                 }
             }
         }

codex
The main scaling limit is synchronization between pivots. Immutable pivot-row snapshots let workers advance independently while preserving the original update order, and they passed the same full-matrix comparisons. I’m testing cache blocking around those snapshots to improve performance on larger matrices.

static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {\\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {\\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {\\n                const                     std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                    const size_t numNodes) {

+    unsigned int* const distances = dist.data();
+    unsigned int* const paths = path.data();
+
+    // Keep the team alive across all pivots. Each thread owns complete rows;
+    // the worksharing barrier finishes pivot k before any thread starts k + 1.
+    #pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {

+

+            for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
+                // With nonnegative weights and a zero diagonal, the pivot row
+                // cannot improve. Leaving it untouched makes shared reads safe.
+                if (i == k) {
+                    continue;
+                }
+                unsigned int* const row = distances + i * numNodes;
+                unsigned int* const pathRow = paths + i * numNodes;
+                const unsigned int distIK = row[k];
+
+                #pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
+                    const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivot[j];
+                    if (newDist < row[j]) {
+                        row[j] = newDist;
+                        pathRow[j] = intermediate;
+                    }

                    std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                    const size_t numNodes) {

+    // the worksharing barrier finishes pivot k before any thread starts k + 1.
+    #pragma omp parallel

import os, re, subprocess
for n in [8192, 16384]:
    for variant in ['pipeline', 'blocked', 'tile-16-128', 'tile-16-256', 'tile-32-128']:
        for threads in [64, 128]:
            env = dict(os.environ, OMP_NUM_THREADS=str(threads), OMP_PROC_BIND='close', OMP_PLACES='cores')
            try:
                result = subprocess.check_output(['/tmp/floydwarshall-' + variant, '-n', str(n), '-v', '-r'], env=env, text=True, timeout=45)
                timing = re.search(r'Computation time: (\\d+)', result).group(1)
                checksum = re.search(r'Hash: (\\w+)', result).group(1)
                print(f'n={n} {variant} threads={threads}: {timing} ms hash={checksum}', flush=True)
            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                print(f'n={n} {variant} threads={threads}: exceeded 45 seconds', flush=True)

codex
The comparisons favor streaming row updates when each worker has a modest amount of data, and column tiling when its working set is larger. I’m combining those paths under OpenMP, with a cap on workers for small graphs. The final checks will compare both output matrices and exercise partial blocks under sanitizers.

+namespace {
+// Avoid idle workers and synchronization dominating small graphs.
+int openmpThreadCount(const size_t numNodes) noexcept {
+    const size_t available = static_cast<size_t>(
+        std::min(omp_get_max_threads(), omp_get_num_procs()));
+    return static_cast<int>(std::min(available, std::max(size_t{1}, numNodes / 16)));
+}
+
+inline void relaxRow(unsigned int* const row, unsigned int* const pathRow,
+                     const unsigned int* const pivot, const unsigned int distIK,
+                     const unsigned int intermediate, const size_t begin,
+                     const size_t end) noexcept {
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+        const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivot[j];
+        if (newDist < row[j]) {
+            row[j] = newDist;
+            pathRow[j] = intermediate;
+        }
+    }

 void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                    std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                    const size_t numNodes) {
-    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
-    // For each intermediate node k
-    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
-        // For each source node i
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
-            // For each destination node j
-            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
-                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
-                
-                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
-                
-                if (newDist < distIJ) {
-                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
-                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
+    constexpr size_t streamPivotBlockSize = 32;
+    constexpr size_t cachePivotBlockSize = 16;
+    constexpr size_t columnBlockSize = 256;
+
+    // Each snapshot is the pivot row after exactly k preceding pivots.
+    // Immutable snapshots let workers advance without a barrier at every k.
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotRows(streamPivotBlockSize * numNodes);
+    struct alignas(64) PivotReady {
+        std::atomic<size_t> value{0};
+    };
+    PivotReady ready[streamPivotBlockSize];
+    unsigned int* const distances = dist.data();
+    unsigned int* const paths = path.data();
+    const int teamSize = openmpThreadCount(numNodes);
+
+    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(teamSize)
+    {
+        const size_t threads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_num_threads());
+        const size_t thread = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());
+        const size_t rowsPerThread = numNodes / threads;
+        const size_t extraRows = numNodes % threads;
+        const size_t firstRow = thread * rowsPerThread + std::min(thread, extraRows);
+        const size_t lastRow = firstRow + rowsPerThread + (thread < extraRows);
+
+        // Stream rows while their working set is small; otherwise keep a
+        // column tile in cache across several ordered pivots.
+        const bool cacheBlocked = numNodes >= 4096 &&
+                                  rowsPerThread * numNodes >= 1024 * 1024;
+        const size_t pivotBlockSize = cacheBlocked ? cachePivotBlockSize
+                                                  : streamPivotBlockSize;
+
+        for (size_t firstPivot = 0; firstPivot < numNodes; firstPivot += pivotBlockSize) {
+            const size_t lastPivot = std::min(numNodes, firstPivot + pivotBlockSize);
+
+            if (!cacheBlocked) {
+                for (size_t k = firstPivot; k < lastPivot; ++k) {
+                    const size_t slot = k - firstPivot;
+                    unsigned int* const pivot = pivotRows.data() + slot * numNodes;
+                    if (k >= firstRow && k < lastRow) {
+                        std::copy_n(distances + k * numNodes, numNodes, pivot);
+                        ready[slot].value.store(k + 1, std::memory_order_release);
+                    } else {
+                        while (ready[slot].value.load(std::memory_order_acquire) != k + 1) {
+                        }
+                    }
+                    for (size_t i = firstRow; i < lastRow; ++i) {
+                        // Zero diagonal and nonnegative benchmark weights mean
+                        // the pivot row never improves during its own step.
+                        if (i == k) {
+                            continue;
+                        }
+                        unsigned int* const row = distances + i * numNodes;
+                        relaxRow(row, paths + i * numNodes, pivot, row[k],
+                                 static_cast<unsigned int>(k), 0, numNodes);
+                    }
+                }
+            } else {
+                // Distribute the block's pivot rows cyclically. Only these
+                // rows must be advanced before later snapshots can be made.
+                if (thread < lastPivot - firstPivot) {
+                    for (size_t k = firstPivot; k < lastPivot; ++k) {
+                        const size_t slot = k - firstPivot;
+                        unsigned int* const pivot = pivotRows.data() + slot * numNodes;
+                        if (slot % threads == thread) {
+                            std::copy_n(distances + k * numNodes, numNodes, pivot);
+                            ready[slot].value.store(k + 1, std::memory_order_release);
+                        } else {
+                            while (ready[slot].value.load(std::memory_order_acquire) != k + 1) {
+                            }
+                        }
+                        for (size_t i = firstPivot + thread; i < lastPivot; i += threads) {
+                            if (i == k) {
+                                continue;
+                            }
+                            unsigned int* const row = distances + i * numNodes;
+                            relaxRow(row, paths + i * numNodes, pivot, row[k],
+                                     static_cast<unsigned int>(k), 0, numNodes);
+                        }
+                    }
                 }
+
+                for (size_t i = firstRow; i < lastRow; ++i) {
+                    if (i >= firstPivot && i < lastPivot) {
+                        continue;
+                    }
+                    unsigned int* const row = distances + i * numNodes;
+                    unsigned int* const pathRow = paths + i * numNodes;
+                    unsigned int distToPivot[cachePivotBlockSize];
+
+                    // First advance the pivot columns, saving dist[i][k] at
+                    // each original k step. Other columns cannot affect them.
+                    for (size_t k = firstPivot; k < lastPivot; ++k) {
+                        const size_t slot = k - firstPivot;
+                        while (ready[slot].value.load(std::memory_order_acquire) != k + 1) {
+                        }
+                        const unsigned int* const pivot = pivotRows.data() + slot * numNodes;
+                        distToPivot[slot] = row[k];
+                        relaxRow(row, pathRow, pivot, distToPivot[slot],
+                                 static_cast<unsigned int>(k), firstPivot, lastPivot);
+                    }
+
+                    // Reuse each distance/path tile for every pivot in the
+                    // block. Strict comparisons and ascending k preserve ties
+                    // and the original path matrix, as well as distances.
+                    for (size_t range = 0; range < 2; ++range) {
+                        const size_t begin = range == 0 ? 0 : lastPivot;
+                        const size_t end = range == 0 ? firstPivot : numNodes;
+                        for (size_t firstColumn = begin; firstColumn < end;
+                             firstColumn += columnBlockSize) {
+                            const size_t lastColumn = std::min(end, firstColumn + columnBlockSize);
+                            for (size_t k = firstPivot; k < lastPivot; ++k) {
+                                const size_t slot = k - firstPivot;
+                                const unsigned int* const pivot = pivotRows.data() + slot * numNodes;
+                                relaxRow(row, pathRow, pivot, distToPivot[slot],
+                                         static_cast<unsigned int>(k), firstColumn, lastColumn);
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
             }
+            // Finish every row before reusing snapshots or changing row owners.
+            #pragma omp barrier
         }
     }
+
 }
 
 bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {

+                        unsigned int* const row = distances + i * numNodes;
+                        relaxRow(row, paths + i * numNodes, pivot, row[k],
+                                 static_cast<unsigned int>(k), 0, numNodes);
+                    }
+                }
+            } else {
+                // Distribute the block's pivot rows cyclically. Only these
+                // rows must be advanced before later snapshots can be made.
+                if (thread < lastPivot - firstPivot) {
+                    for (size_t k = firstPivot; k < lastPivot; ++k) {
+                        const size_t slot = k - firstPivot;
+                        unsigned int* const pivot = pivotRows.data() + slot * numNodes;
+                        if (slot % threads == thread) {
+                            std::copy_n(distances + k * numNodes, numNodes, pivot);
+                            ready[slot].value.store(k + 1, std::memory_order_release);
+                        } else {
+                            while (ready[slot].value.load(std::memory_order_acquire) != k + 1) {
+                            }
+                        }
+                        for (size_t i = firstPivot + thread; i < lastPivot; i += threads) {
+                            if (i == k) {
+                                continue;
+                            }
+                            unsigned int* const row = distances + i * numNodes;
+                            relaxRow(row, paths + i * numNodes, pivot, row[k],
+                                     static_cast<unsigned int>(k), 0, numNodes);
+                        }
+                    }
                 }
+
+                for (size_t i = firstRow; i < lastRow; ++i) {
+                    if (i >= firstPivot && i < lastPivot) {
+                        continue;
+                    }
+                    unsigned int* const row = distances + i * numNodes;
+                    unsigned int* const pathRow = paths + i * numNodes;
+                    unsigned int distToPivot[cachePivotBlockSize];
+
+                    // First advance the pivot columns, saving dist[i][k] at
+                    // each original k step. Other columns cannot affect them.
+                    for (size_t k = firstPivot; k < lastPivot; ++k) {
+                        const size_t slot = k - firstPivot;
+                        while (ready[slot].value.load(std::memory_order_acquire) != k + 1) {
+                        }
+                        const unsigned int* const pivot = pivotRows.data() + slot * numNodes;
+                        distToPivot[slot] = row[k];
+                        relaxRow(row, pathRow, pivot, distToPivot[slot],
+                                 static_cast<unsigned int>(k), firstPivot, lastPivot);
+                    }
+
+                    // Reuse each distance/path tile for every pivot in the
+                    // block. Strict comparisons and ascending k preserve ties
+                    // and the original path matrix, as well as distances.
+                    for (size_t range = 0; range < 2; ++range) {
+                        const size_t begin = range == 0 ? 0 : lastPivot;
+                        const size_t end = range == 0 ? firstPivot : numNodes;
+                        for (size_t firstColumn = begin; firstColumn < end;
+                             firstColumn += columnBlockSize) {
+                            const size_t lastColumn = std::min(end, firstColumn + columnBlockSize);
+                            for (size_t k = firstPivot; k < lastPivot; ++k) {
+                                const size_t slot = k - firstPivot;
+                                const unsigned int* const pivot = pivotRows.data() + slot * numNodes;
+                                relaxRow(row, pathRow, pivot, distToPivot[slot],
+                                         static_cast<unsigned int>(k), firstColumn, lastColumn);
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
             }
+            // Finish every row before reusing snapshots or changing row owners.
+            #pragma omp barrier
         }
     }
+
 }

+namespace {
+// Avoid idle workers and synchronization dominating small graphs.
+int openmpThreadCount(const size_t numNodes) noexcept {
+    const size_t available = static_cast<size_t>(
+        std::min(omp_get_max_threads(), omp_get_num_procs()));
+    return static_cast<int>(std::min(available, std::max(size_t{1}, numNodes / 16)));
+}
+
+inline void relaxRow(unsigned int* const row, unsigned int* const pathRow,
+                     const unsigned int* const pivot, const unsigned int distIK,
+                     const unsigned int intermediate, const size_t begin,
+                     const size_t end) noexcept {
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+        const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivot[j];
+        if (newDist < row[j]) {
+            row[j] = newDist;
+            pathRow[j] = intermediate;
+        }
+    }
+}

 void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                    std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                    const size_t numNodes) {
-    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
-    // For each intermediate node k
-    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
-        // For each source node i
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
-            // For each destination node j
-            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
-                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
-                
-                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
-                
-                if (newDist < distIJ) {
-                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
-                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
+    constexpr size_t streamPivotBlockSize = 32;
+    constexpr size_t cachePivotBlockSize = 16;
+    constexpr size_t columnBlockSize = 256;
+
+    // Each snapshot is the pivot row after exactly k preceding pivots.
+    // Immutable snapshots let workers advance without a barrier at every k.
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotRows(streamPivotBlockSize * numNodes);
+    struct alignas(64) PivotReady {
+        std::atomic<size_t> value{0};
+    };
+    PivotReady ready[streamPivotBlockSize];
+    unsigned int* const distances = dist.data();
+    unsigned int* const paths = path.data();
+    const int teamSize = openmpThreadCount(numNodes);
+
+    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(teamSize)
+    {
+        const size_t threads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_num_threads());
+        const size_t thread = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());
+        const size_t rowsPerThread = numNodes / threads;
+        const size_t extraRows = numNodes % threads;
+        const size_t firstRow = thread * rowsPerThread + std::min(thread, extraRows);
+        const size_t lastRow = firstRow + rowsPerThread + (thread < extraRows);
+
+        // Stream rows while their working set is small; otherwise keep a
+        // column tile in cache across several ordered pivots.
+        const bool cacheBlocked = numNodes >= 4096 &&
+                                  rowsPerThread * numNodes >= 1024 * 1024;
+        const size_t pivotBlockSize = cacheBlocked ? cachePivotBlockSize
+                                                  : streamPivotBlockSize;
+
+        for (size_t firstPivot = 0; firstPivot < numNodes; firstPivot += pivotBlockSize) {
+            const size_t lastPivot = std::min(numNodes, firstPivot + pivotBlockSize);
+
+            if (!cacheBlocked) {
+                for (size_t k = firstPivot; k < lastPivot; ++k) {
+                    const size_t slot = k - firstPivot;
+                    unsigned int* const pivot = pivotRows.data() + slot * numNodes;
+                    if (k >= firstRow && k < lastRow) {
+                        std::copy_n(distances + k * numNodes, numNodes, pivot);
+                        ready[slot].value.store(k + 1, std::memory_order_release);
+                    } else {
+                        while (ready[slot].value.load(std::memory_order_acquire) != k + 1) {
+                        }
+                    }
+                    for (size_t i = firstRow; i < lastRow; ++i) {
+                        // Zero diagonal and nonnegative benchmark weights mean
+                        // the pivot row never improves during its own step.
+                        if (i == k) {
+                            continue;
+                        }
+                        unsigned int* const row = distances + i * numNodes;
+                        relaxRow(row, paths + i * numNodes, pivot, row[k],
+                                 static_cast<unsigned int>(k), 0, numNodes);
+                    }
+                }
+            } else {
+                // Distribute the block's pivot rows cyclically. Only these
+                // rows must be advanced before later snapshots can be made.
+                if (thread < lastPivot - firstPivot) {
+                    for (size_t k = firstPivot; k < lastPivot; ++k) {
+                        const size_t slot = k - firstPivot;
+                        unsigned int* const pivot = pivotRows.data() + slot * numNodes;
+                        if (slot % threads == thread) {
+                            std::copy_n(distances + k * numNodes, numNodes, pivot);
+                            ready[slot].value.store(k + 1, std::memory_order_release);
+                        } else {
+                            while (ready[slot].value.load(std::memory_order_acquire) != k + 1) {
+                            }
+                        }
+                        for (size_t i = firstPivot + thread; i < lastPivot; i += threads) {
+                            if (i == k) {
+                                continue;
+                            }
+                            unsigned int* const row = distances + i * numNodes;
+                            relaxRow(row, paths + i * numNodes, pivot, row[k],
+                                     static_cast<unsigned int>(k), 0, numNodes);
+                        }
+                    }
                 }
+
+                for (size_t i = firstRow; i < lastRow; ++i) {
+                    if (i >= firstPivot && i < lastPivot) {
+                        continue;
+                    }
+                    unsigned int* const row = distances + i * numNodes;
+                    unsigned int* const pathRow = paths + i * numNodes;
+                    unsigned int distToPivot[cachePivotBlockSize];
+
+                    // First advance the pivot columns, saving dist[i][k] at
+                    // each original k step. Other columns cannot affect them.
+                    for (size_t k = firstPivot; k < lastPivot; ++k) {
+                        const size_t slot = k - firstPivot;
+                        while (ready[slot].value.load(std::memory_order_acquire) != k + 1) {
+                        }
+                        const unsigned int* const pivot = pivotRows.data() + slot * numNodes;
+                        distToPivot[slot] = row[k];
+                        relaxRow(row, pathRow, pivot, distToPivot[slot],
+                                 static_cast<unsigned int>(k), firstPivot, lastPivot);
+                    }
+
+                    // Reuse each distance/path tile for every pivot in the
+                    // block. Strict comparisons and ascending k preserve ties
+                    // and the original path matrix, as well as distances.
+                    for (size_t range = 0; range < 2; ++range) {
+                        const size_t begin = range == 0 ? 0 : lastPivot;
+                        const size_t end = range == 0 ? firstPivot : numNodes;
+                        for (size_t firstColumn = begin; firstColumn < end;
+                             firstColumn += columnBlockSize) {
+                            const size_t lastColumn = std::min(end, firstColumn + columnBlockSize);
+                            for (size_t k = firstPivot; k < lastPivot; ++k) {
+                                const size_t slot = k - firstPivot;
+                                const unsigned int* const pivot = pivotRows.data() + slot * numNodes;
+                                relaxRow(row, pathRow, pivot, distToPivot[slot],
+                                         static_cast<unsigned int>(k), firstColumn, lastColumn);
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
             }
+            // Finish every row before reusing snapshots or changing row owners.
+            #pragma omp barrier
         }
     }
+
 }

+    constexpr size_t pivotBlockSize = 32;
+    constexpr size_t columnBlockSize = 256;
+
+    // Each snapshot is the pivot row after exactly k preceding pivots.
+    // Immutable snapshots let workers advance without a barrier at every k.
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotRows(pivotBlockSize * numNodes);
+    struct alignas(64) PivotReady {
+        std::atomic<size_t> value{0};
+    };
+    PivotReady ready[pivotBlockSize];
+    unsigned int* const distances = dist.data();
+    unsigned int* const paths = path.data();
+    const int teamSize = openmpThreadCount(numNodes);
+
+    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(teamSize)
+    {
+        const size_t threads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_num_threads());
+        const size_t thread = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());
+        const size_t rowsPerThread = numNodes / threads;
+        const size_t extraRows = numNodes % threads;
+        const size_t firstRow = thread * rowsPerThread + std::min(thread, extraRows);
+        const size_t lastRow = firstRow + rowsPerThread + (thread < extraRows);
+
+        // Stream rows while their working set is small; otherwise keep a
+        // column tile in cache across several ordered pivots.
+        const bool cacheBlocked = numNodes >= 4096 &&
+                                  rowsPerThread * numNodes >= 1024 * 1024;
+
+        for (size_t firstPivot = 0; firstPivot < numNodes; firstPivot += pivotBlockSize) {
+            const size_t lastPivot = std::min(numNodes, firstPivot + pivotBlockSize);
+
+            if (!cacheBlocked) {
+                for (size_t k = firstPivot; k < lastPivot; ++k) {
+                    const size_t slot = k - firstPivot;
+                    unsigned int* const pivot = pivotRows.data() + slot * numNodes;
+                    if (k >= firstRow && k < lastRow) {
+                        std::copy_n(distances + k * numNodes, numNodes, pivot);
+                        ready[slot].value.store(k + 1, std::memory_order_release);
+                    } else {
+                        while (ready[slot].value.load(std::memory_order_acquire) != k + 1) {
+                        }
+                    }
+                    for (size_t i = firstRow; i < lastRow; ++i) {
+                        // With the benchmark's zero diagonal, the pivot row
+                        // never improves during its own step.
+                        if (i == k) {
+                            continue;
+                        }
+                        unsigned int* const row = distances + i * numNodes;
+                        relaxRow(row, paths + i * numNodes, pivot, row[k],
+                                 static_cast<unsigned int>(k), 0, numNodes);
+                    }
+                }
+            } else {
+                // Distribute the block's pivot rows cyclically. Only these
+                // rows must be advanced before later snapshots can be made.
+                if (thread < lastPivot - firstPivot) {
+                    for (size_t k = firstPivot; k < lastPivot; ++k) {
+                        const size_t slot = k - firstPivot;
+                        unsigned int* const pivot = pivotRows.data() + slot * numNodes;
+                        if (slot % threads == thread) {
+                            std::copy_n(distances + k * numNodes, numNodes, pivot);
+                            ready[slot].value.store(k + 1, std::memory_order_release);
+                        } else {
+                            while (ready[slot].value.load(std::memory_order_acquire) != k + 1) {
+                            }
+                        }
+                        for (size_t i = firstPivot + thread; i < lastPivot; i += threads) {
+                            if (i == k) {
+                                continue;
+                            }
+                            unsigned int* const row = distances + i * numNodes;
+                            relaxRow(row, paths + i * numNodes, pivot, row[k],
+                                     static_cast<unsigned int>(k), 0, numNodes);
+                        }
+                    }
                 }
+
+                for (size_t i = firstRow; i < lastRow; ++i) {
+                    if (i >= firstPivot && i < lastPivot) {
+                        continue;
+                    }
+                    unsigned int* const row = distances + i * numNodes;
+                    unsigned int* const pathRow = paths + i * numNodes;
+                    unsigned int distToPivot[pivotBlockSize];
+
+                    // First advance the pivot columns, saving dist[i][k] at
+                    // each original k step. Other columns cannot affect them.
+                    for (size_t k = firstPivot; k < lastPivot; ++k) {
+                        const size_t slot = k - firstPivot;
+                        while (ready[slot].value.load(std::memory_order_acquire) != k + 1) {
+                        }
+                        const unsigned int* const pivot = pivotRows.data() + slot * numNodes;
+                        distToPivot[slot] = row[k];
+                        relaxRow(row, pathRow, pivot, distToPivot[slot],
+                                 static_cast<unsigned int>(k), firstPivot, lastPivot);
+                    }
+
+                    // Reuse each distance/path tile for every pivot in the
+                    // block. Strict comparisons and ascending k preserve ties
+                    // and the original path matrix, as well as distances.
+                    for (size_t range = 0; range < 2; ++range) {
+                        const size_t begin = range == 0 ? 0 : lastPivot;
+                        const size_t end = range == 0 ? firstPivot : numNodes;
+                        for (size_t firstColumn = begin; firstColumn < end;
+                             firstColumn += columnBlockSize) {
+                            const size_t lastColumn = std::min(end, firstColumn + columnBlockSize);
+                            for (size_t k = firstPivot; k < lastPivot; ++k) {
+                                const size_t slot = k - firstPivot;
+                                const unsigned int* const pivot = pivotRows.data() + slot * numNodes;
+                                relaxRow(row, pathRow, pivot, distToPivot[slot],
+                                         static_cast<unsigned int>(k), firstColumn, lastColumn);
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
             }
+            // Finish every row before reusing snapshots or changing row owners.
+            #pragma omp barrier
         }
     }
+
 }
